		function buildBannerObj() {
			var banner = {};
			var args = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments, 0);

			args.forEach(function (creativeObj) {

				var obj = creativeObj,
					count = 1;

				if (creativeObj.custom) {
					obj = creativeObj.custom;
					count = creativeObj.count
				}

				if (banner[obj.name]) return;

				banner[obj.name] = {
					creative: obj,
					count: count
				};

			});

			return banner;
		}

			(function(googletag_config, adUnitPath, slotId, keyValues, module, exposeFnName) {

				var availableSlots = {};

				function getAllSlotsParams(adPlacements) {

					var defineSlotArgs = [];

					Object.keys(adPlacements).forEach(function (key) {

						var placement = adPlacements[key];
						var sizes = placement.creative.sizes[device];
						if (sizes === null) return;


						if (placement.count > 1) {
							for (var i = 1; i <= placement.count; i++) {
								defineSlotArgs.push({
									name: placement.creative.name,
									adUnitPath: '/' + slotId + '/' + adUnitPath + '/' + key,
									size: sizes,
									optDiv: 'ad-' + key + '-' + i + '-' + device[0]
								});
							}
						} else {
							defineSlotArgs.push({
								name: placement.creative.name,
								adUnitPath: '/' + slotId + '/' + adUnitPath + '/' + key,
								size: sizes,
								optDiv: 'ad-' + key + '-' + device[0]
							});
						}
					});

					return defineSlotArgs
				}

				function initAllSlots(defineSlotArgs) {

					defineSlotArgs.forEach(function (defineSlotparam) {

						var slot;

						if (defineSlotparam.size) {
							logger.log('- - -adplacements setup: ', defineSlotparam.adUnitPath, defineSlotparam.size, defineSlotparam.optDiv);

							if (defineSlotparam.name === 'interstitiel') {
								slot = googletag.defineOutOfPageSlot(defineSlotparam.adUnitPath, defineSlotparam.optDiv).addService(googletag.pubads());
							} else {
								slot = googletag.defineSlot(defineSlotparam.adUnitPath, defineSlotparam.size, defineSlotparam.optDiv).addService(googletag.pubads());
							}
						}

						if (slot) {
							availableSlots[defineSlotparam.optDiv] = slot;
						}
					});
				}
				
				function initKeyValues(keyValues) {
					for (var key in keyValues) {
						if (keyValues.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
							googletag.pubads().setTargeting(key, keyValues[key]);
						}
					}

					logger.log('- - -key-values setup: ', keyValues);
				}

				function isValidParams(args) {
					if (args.length < 3) {
						logger.error('not all params provided: expect config, adUnitPath, slotId, keyValues[optional]');
						return false;
					}

					return true
				}

				function refreshSlot(slotId) {
					var slot = availableSlots[slotId];
					if(typeof googletag.pubads === 'undefined' || !slot) return;

					var shouldRefresh = document.getElementById(slot.getSlotElementId()).className.indexOf('no-refresh') === -1;
					if(shouldRefresh) {
						slot.setTargeting('autorefresh', 'on');
						googletag.pubads().clear([slot]);
						googletag.pubads().refresh([slot]);
					}
				}

				window[module] = window[module] || {};
				window[module][exposeFnName] = refreshSlot;

		(function tagmanagerAdsDisplay(googleTagConfig, device, module, exposeFnName) {
			var availableSlots = Object.keys(googleTagConfig).reduce(function(acc, curr) {
				var entity = googleTagConfig[curr];
				acc[entity.creative.name] = acc[entity.creative.name] || { totalCount: entity.count, currentCount: 1};
				return acc;
			}, {});

			function initAdPlaceholder(options) {

				var slotId = options.slotId,
					refresh = options.refresh,
					placementId = options.placementId,
					el = document.querySelector("[data-adplacement-id='" + placementId + "']");

				if (el === null) return;

				el.className = el.getAttribute('data-class');
				el.id = slotId;

				if (refresh) {
						window.__es_gtm_helper.refresh_ad(slotId);
				} else {
					googletag.cmd.push(function() {
						googletag.display(slotId)
					});
				}
			}

			function injectAd(adSlotType, placementId, isNoMobile, isNoTablet, isNoDesktop) {
				var adConfig = availableSlots[adSlotType] || null,
					slotId;

				if (adConfig === null ) return;

				if (isNoMobile && device === 'mobile-web') return;
				if (isNoTablet && device === 'tablet-web') return;
				if (isNoDesktop && device === 'desktop') return;


				if (adConfig.currentCount > adConfig.totalCount) {

					for(var i = 0; i < adConfig.totalCount; i++) {
						var _idx =  (adConfig.totalCount > 1 ? '-'+adConfig.currentCount : '') + '-' +device[0];
						var _slotId = 'ad-'+adSlotType + _idx ;
						if (document.getElementById(_slotId) === null) {
							slotId = _slotId;
							break;
						}
					}

					if (slotId) {
						initAdPlaceholder({placementId: placementId, slotId: slotId, refresh: true})
					}
				} else {

					var adIndex  = (adConfig.totalCount > 1 ? '-'+adConfig.currentCount : '') + '-' +device[0];
					var slotId = 'ad-'+adSlotType + adIndex;
					adConfig.currentCount++;

					initAdPlaceholder({placementId: placementId, slotId: slotId})
				}



			}

			window[module] = window[module] || {};
			window[module][exposeFnName] = injectAd;


		})(googletag_config, device,'__es_gtm_helper','inject_ad');
		
		function Logger(moduleName, isDebug) {

			var _noop = function () {};
			var _isDebug = typeof isDebug === 'undefined' ? true : isDebug;
			this.log = _noop;
			this.error = _noop;


			if (_isDebug) {
				this.log = console.log.bind(console, moduleName);
				this.error = console.error.bind(console, moduleName);
			}
		}

function initializeHeartbeat(e) {
	return new Heartbeat(e);
}

  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  // Utils
	utils = {
	  getUrlVars: function () {
		var vars = [], hash;
		var hashes = window.location.href.slice(window.location.href.indexOf('?') + 1).split('&');

		for (var i = 0; i < hashes.length; i++) {
		  hash = hashes[i].split('=');
		  hash[1] = unescape(hash[1]);
		  vars.push(hash[0]);
		  vars[hash[0]] = hash[1];
		}

		return vars;
	  }
	};


  ///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  // Logger
  var Logger = function () {

	var urlVars = utils.getUrlVars();
	this._ = {
	  enabled: (urlVars['heartbeat-debug'] == 1)
	};

  };
  Logger.prototype = {
	isEnabled: function () {
	  return this._.enabled;
	},
	_date: function () {
	  var date = new Date();
	  var hours = ('0' + date.getHours()).slice(-2);
	  var minutes = ('0' + date.getMinutes()).slice(-2);
	  var seconds = ('0' + date.getSeconds()).slice(-2);
	  var milliseconds = ('00' + date.getMilliseconds()).slice(-3);

	  return '[' + hours + ':' + minutes + ':' + seconds + '.' + milliseconds + ']';
	},
	log: function () {
	  if (!this._.enabled) return;

	  var args = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments);
	  args.unshift(this._date());

	  console.log.apply(console, args);
	}
  };

  var logger = new Logger();

var Heartbeat = function(e) {
	// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  Initialize', e);
	
	this._ = {
		//ADB
		MediaHeartbeat         : ADB.va.MediaHeartbeat,
		
		// Objects
		mediaHeartbeat         : null,
		playerController       : null,
		playerConfig		   : null,
		analyticsConfig        : null,
		videoMeta              : null,
		dataLayer              : null,
		streamType             : null,
		duration               : null,
		isAnalyticsApiEnabled  : false,	
		isJwPlayer			   : false,
		isAdBreakStartTrigger  : false,
		
		//Properties
		isTrackingSession 	   : false,
		isSeekStart            : false,
		isInitialPlay          : true,
		podPosition            : 0,
		
		// Callbacks
		callbackPlay: this._callbackPlay.bind(this),
		callbackEnded: this._callbackEnded.bind(this),
		callbackPause: this._callbackPause.bind(this),
		callbackSeek: this._callbackSeek.bind(this),
		callbackSeeked: this._callbackSeeked.bind(this),
		callbackAdsAdSkip: this._callbackAdsAdSkip.bind(this),
		callbackAdsPodStarted: this._callbackAdsPodStarted.bind(this),
		callbackAdsPodEnded: this._callbackAdsPodEnded.bind(this),
		callbackAdsAdStarted: this._callbackAdsAdStarted.bind(this),
		callbackAdsAdEnded: this._callbackAdsAdEnded.bind(this)
	};

	// Check detail is defined
	if (typeof e.detail == 'undefined') return false;
	
	// Get Metadatas
	this._.playerController = e.detail.playerController;
	this._.analyticsConfig = this._.playerController.getAnalyticsConfig();
	this._.playerConfig = this._.analyticsConfig.getPlayerConfig();
	this._.videoMeta = this._.analyticsConfig.getVideoMeta();
	this._.dataLayer = this._.analyticsConfig.getDataLayer();
	this._.isAnalyticsApiEnabled = this._.analyticsConfig.isAnalyticsApiEnabled();
			
	//Media Heartbeat initialization
	var MediaHeartbeatConfig = ADB.va.MediaHeartbeatConfig;
	var mediaConfig = new MediaHeartbeatConfig();
	mediaConfig.trackingServer = "discoverydp.hb.omtrdc.net";
	mediaConfig.trackingServerSecure = "discoverydp.hb.omtrdc.net";
	mediaConfig.playerName = this._.playerConfig.playerName;
	if (this._.playerConfig.playerName == 'JWPlayer' || !this._.isAnalyticsApiEnabled) {
		mediaConfig.channel = this._.playerConfig.channel || "freevod";
	}
	else {
		mediaConfig.channel = this._.playerConfig.channel;
	}
	mediaConfig.debugLogging = (this._.playerConfig.env !== 'production' && this._.playerConfig.env !== 'prod');
	mediaConfig.appVersion = this._.playerConfig.appVersion;
	mediaConfig.ssl = (document.location.protocol === "https:");

	//Media Delegate
	var MediaHeartbeatDelegate = ADB.va.MediaHeartbeatDelegate;
	var mediaDelegate = new MediaHeartbeatDelegate();
	var self = this;
	mediaDelegate.getCurrentPlaybackTime = function() {
		
		// Ad tracking
		var currentAdData = self._.playerController.getCurrentAdData();
		var isRunningAd = currentAdData != null && currentAdData.currentTime != null && currentAdData.currentTime != -1;
		if (isRunningAd) { 
			return Number(currentAdData.currentTime);
		}
		
		// Video content tracking
		return Number(self._.playerController.getCurrentTime());
	};
	
	// Get the app measurement instance.
	var counter = 0;
	var timer = setInterval(function(){
		if (counter == 200) {
			clearInterval(timer);
		}
		else if(typeof window.s_c_il !== 'undefined' && window.s_c_il[1] != null) {
			counter++;
			var appMeasurement = window.s_c_il[1];
			self._.mediaHeartbeat = new self._.MediaHeartbeat(mediaDelegate, mediaConfig, appMeasurement);		
			self.initEvents();
			clearInterval(timer);			
		}
	},100)

  // if (appMeasurement != null) {
    // this._.mediaHeartbeat = new this._.MediaHeartbeat(mediaDelegate, mediaConfig, appMeasurement);
    // this.initEvents();
  // }
};
	
Heartbeat.prototype = {
	initEvents: function() {
		
		this._.playerController.on('play', this._.callbackPlay);
		this._.playerController.one('ended',this._.callbackEnded);
		this._.playerController.on('pause', this._.callbackPause);
		this._.playerController.on('seek', this._.callbackSeek);
		this._.playerController.on('seeked', this._.callbackSeeked);
		this._.playerController.on('ads-ad-skip', this._.callbackAdsAdSkip);
		this._.playerController.on('ads-pod-started', this._.callbackAdsPodStarted);
		this._.playerController.on('ads-pod-ended', this._.callbackAdsPodEnded);
		this._.playerController.on('ads-ad-started', this._.callbackAdsAdStarted);
		this._.playerController.on('ads-ad-ended', this._.callbackAdsAdEnded);		
	},
	_callbackPlay: function(){
		// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  Play *** for video : ', this._.videoMeta.title, this._);
		
		this._checkSessionStart();
		
		//On video play
		if (!this._.isSeekStart) {
			this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackPlay();
		}		
	},	
	_callbackEnded: function(){
		// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  Complete *** for video : ', this._.videoMeta.title, this._);
		this._checkSessionStart();
		
		//On video complete
		this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackComplete();
		this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackSessionEnd();
		this._.isInitialPlay = true;
		this._.isTrackingSession = false;
		
		this._destroyEvents();
	},
	_callbackPause: function(){
		// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  Pause *** for video : ', this._.videoMeta.title, this._);
		
		this._checkSessionStart();
		
		//On video complete
		if (!this._.isSeekStart) {
			this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackPause();
		}		
	},	
	_callbackSeek: function(){
		// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  Seek Start *** for video : ', this._.videoMeta.title, this._);
		
		this._checkSessionStart();
			
		//On seek start
		this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackEvent(this._.MediaHeartbeat.Event.SeekStart);
		this._.isSeekStart = true;		
	},
	_callbackSeeked: function(){
		// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  Seek Complete *** for video : ', this._.videoMeta.title, this._);
		
		this._checkSessionStart();
		
		//On seek complete
		this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackEvent(this._.MediaHeartbeat.Event.SeekComplete);
		this._.isSeekStart = false;		
	},	
	_callbackAdsAdSkip: function(){
		// logger.log('[Heartbeat] Ad Skip  *** for video : ', this._.videoMeta.title, this._);
		
		this._checkSessionStart();
		
		// On ad skip 
		//this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackEvent(this._.MediaHeartbeat.Event.AdSkip);
	},
	_callbackAdsPodStarted: function(){
		// logger.log('[Heartbeat] Ad Break start *** for video : ', this._.videoMeta.title, this._);
	
		this._checkSessionStart();
		
		var currentAdData = this._.playerController.getCurrentAdData();
		if (currentAdData != null) {		
			var adBreakObject = this._.MediaHeartbeat.createAdBreakObject(currentAdData.type, ++this._.podPosition, 0);
			this._isAdBreakStartTrigger = true;
			
			// On ad break start 
			this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackEvent(this._.MediaHeartbeat.Event.AdBreakStart, adBreakObject);
		}		
},
	_callbackAdsPodEnded: function(){
		// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  Ad Break complete *** for video : ', this._.videoMeta.title, this._);
		
		this._checkSessionStart();
		this._isAdBreakStartTrigger = false;
		
		//On Ad Break complete
		this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackEvent(this._.MediaHeartbeat.Event.AdBreakComplete);		
	},	
	_callbackAdsAdStarted: function(){
		
		this._checkSessionStart();
		
		var currentAdData = this._.playerController.getCurrentAdData();
		if (currentAdData != null) {	
			if (!this._isAdBreakStartTrigger) {
			// logger.log('[Heartbeat] Ad Break start *** for video : ', this._.videoMeta.title, this._);
			var adBreakObject = this._.MediaHeartbeat.createAdBreakObject(currentAdData.type, ++this._.podPosition, 0);
			this._isAdBreakStartTrigger = true;
			
			// On ad break start 
			this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackEvent(this._.MediaHeartbeat.Event.AdBreakStart, adBreakObject);
		}
		
		// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  Ad start *** for video : ', this._.videoMeta.title, this._);
		
		
			var adObject = this._.MediaHeartbeat.createAdObject(currentAdData.id, currentAdData.id, currentAdData.index, currentAdData.duration);

			// Create ad custom meta data
			var adCustomMetadata = {
					ad_campaign: "",
					ad_campaign_id: "",
					ad_brand: ""
			};
			
			//On Ad start
			this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackEvent(this._.MediaHeartbeat.Event.AdStart, adObject, adCustomMetadata);
			// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  Ad Play *** for video : ', this._.videoMeta.title, this._);
			this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackPlay();
		}		
	},
	_callbackAdsAdEnded: function(){
		// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  Ad complete *** video : ', this._.videoMeta.title, this._);
		
		this._checkSessionStart();
		
		//On Ad complete
		this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackEvent(this._.MediaHeartbeat.Event.AdComplete);		
	},
	_checkSessionStart: function() {
		if (this._.isInitialPlay) {
			// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  Session start *** video : ', this._.videoMeta.title, this._);
			if (this._.isTrackingSession) {
				// Close previous tracking session
				this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackSessionEnd();
			}
			
			this._.duration = this._.analyticsConfig.getDuration(); //this._.playerController.getDuration();
			this._.isJwPlayer = this._.playerConfig.playerName == 'JWPlayer';
			
			// 1. Get media object
			var mediaObject = this._getMediaObject();
			// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  mediaObject : ', mediaObject);
			
			// 2. Set standard Video Metadata
			var standardVideoMetadata = this._getStandardVideoMetadata();
			// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  standardVideoMetadata : ', standardVideoMetadata);
			mediaObject.setValue(this._.MediaHeartbeat.MediaObjectKey.StandardVideoMetadata, standardVideoMetadata);
			
			// 3. Get custom Video Metadata
			var videoCustomMetadata = this._getVideoCustomMetadata();
			// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  videoCustomMetadata : ', videoCustomMetadata);
			
			// On initial video play. 
			this._.mediaHeartbeat.trackSessionStart(mediaObject, videoCustomMetadata);
			
			this._.isInitialPlay = false;
			this._.isTrackingSession = true;	
			this._.podPosition = 0;	
		}
	},
	_destroyEvents: function() {
		// logger.log('[Heartbeat]  _destroy *** video : ', this._);
		this._.playerController.off('play', this._.callbackPlay);
		this._.playerController.off('ended',this._.callbackEnded);
		this._.playerController.off('pause', this._.callbackPause);
		this._.playerController.off('seek', this._.callbackSeek);
		this._.playerController.off('seeked', this._.callbackSeeked);
		this._.playerController.off('ads-ad-skip', this._.callbackAdsAdSkip);
		this._.playerController.off('ads-pod-started', this._.callbackAdsPodStarted);
		this._.playerController.off('ads-pod-ended', this._.callbackAdsPodEnded);
		this._.playerController.off('ads-ad-started', this._.callbackAdsAdStarted);
		this._.playerController.off('ads-ad-ended', this._.callbackAdsAdEnded);			
	},
	_getMediaObject: function() {
		if (this._.isJwPlayer || !this._.isAnalyticsApiEnabled) {
			this._.streamType = this._.playerController.getAnalyticsStreamType();			
			var streamTypeHB = this._.MediaHeartbeat.StreamType.VOD;
			switch (this._.streamType) {
				case 'linear' :
					streamTypeHB = this._.MediaHeartbeat.StreamType.LINEAR;
					break;
				case 'bonus' :
					streamTypeHB = this._.MediaHeartbeat.StreamType.LINEAR;
					break;
				case 'event_live' :
					streamTypeHB = this._.MediaHeartbeat.StreamType.LIVE;
					break;
			}		
		}
		else {
			var streamTypeHB = this._.videoMeta.streamType;
		}
					
		return this._.MediaHeartbeat.createMediaObject(
			this._.videoMeta.title,
			this._.videoMeta.equinoxeId,
			this._.duration,
			streamTypeHB
		);	
		
	},
	_getStandardVideoMetadata: function() {
		var standardVideoMetadata = {};    
		if (this._.isJwPlayer || !this._.isAnalyticsApiEnabled) {
			standardVideoMetadata[this._.MediaHeartbeat.VideoMetadataKeys.NETWORK] = this._.videoMeta.channelName || "freevod-channel";
		}
		else {
			standardVideoMetadata[this._.MediaHeartbeat.VideoMetadataKeys.NETWORK] = this._.videoMeta.channelName;
		}
		
		return standardVideoMetadata;
	},
	_getVideoCustomMetadata: function() {
		if (this._.isJwPlayer || !this._.isAnalyticsApiEnabled) {			
			return {
				stream_type: this._.streamType,
				transmission_type: this._.videoMeta.transmissionType, 
				station_name: this._.videoMeta.stationName || this._.videoMeta.channelName || "freevod",
				embedded_status: this._.videoMeta.embeddedStatus,
				magazine: this._.videoMeta.magazine,
				family: this._.videoMeta.sportFamily,
				sport: this._.videoMeta.sportName,
				competition: this._.videoMeta.sportCompetition,
				sport_event: this._.videoMeta.sportEvent,
				season: this._.videoMeta.sportSeason,
				round: this._.videoMeta.sportRound,
				discipline: this._.videoMeta.sportDiscipline,
				participants: this._.videoMeta.sportParticipants,
				gender: this._.videoMeta.sportGender,
				sport_age_group: this._.videoMeta.ageGroup,
				// Not provided by JW Player
				provider: this._.videoMeta.provider,
				partner_code: this._.videoMeta.partnerCode,
				topic: this._.videoMeta.topic
			};				
		}
		else {
			return {
				transmission_type: this._.videoMeta.transmissionType || '', 
				embedded_status: this._.videoMeta.embeddedStatus || '',
				magazine: this._.videoMeta.magazine || '',
				family: this._.videoMeta.sportFamily || '',
				sport: this._.videoMeta.sportName || '',
				competition: this._.videoMeta.sportCompetition || '',
				sport_event: this._.videoMeta.sportEvent || '',
				season: this._.videoMeta.sportSeason || '',
				round: this._.videoMeta.sportRound || '',
				discipline: this._.videoMeta.sportDiscipline || '',
				participants: this._.videoMeta.sportParticipants || '',
				gender: this._.videoMeta.sportGender || '',
				offer_type: this._.videoMeta.offerType || '',
				provider: this._.videoMeta.provider || '',
				partner_code: this._.dataLayer.partner_code || '',
				section: this._.dataLayer.service || '',
				product: this._.dataLayer.product || '',
				site: this._.dataLayer.site || '',
				environment: this._.dataLayer.environment || '',
				language_name : this._.dataLayer.language_name || '',
				language_id: this._.dataLayer.language_id || '',
				user_id: this._.dataLayer.user_id || '',
				login_status: this._.dataLayer.login_status || '',
				profile: this._.dataLayer.profile || '',
				content_id: this._.dataLayer.content_id || '',
				perimeter: this._.dataLayer.perimeter || ''
			};				
		}
		
	}
};

window.addEventListener('esv-loaded',  initializeHeartbeat );

function initCustomEvent() {
	if ( typeof window.CustomEvent === "function" ) return false; //If not IE

	function CustomEvent ( event, params ) {
	params = params || { bubbles: false, cancelable: false, detail: undefined };
	var evt = document.createEvent( 'CustomEvent' );
	evt.initCustomEvent( event, params.bubbles, params.cancelable, params.detail );
	return evt;
	}

	CustomEvent.prototype = window.Event.prototype;
	window.CustomEvent = CustomEvent;	
}

// NEW WAY TO MANAGE HEARTBEAT EVENTS IN AN EXTERNAL FILE
// DISPATCH EVENT 'heartbeat-loaded'
var count = 0;
var timer = setInterval(function(){
  if (count == 200) {
    clearInterval(timer);
  }
  else if(typeof window.s_c_il !== 'undefined' && window.s_c_il[1] != null) {
    count++;
	
	// Polyfill IE for CustomEvent
	initCustomEvent();
	
    // Get native javascript object because the listener is set up before jquery is initialized
    var event = new CustomEvent('heartbeat-loaded', { 'detail': {}});
    //distribuer l'événement.
    window.dispatchEvent(event);
    clearInterval(timer);			
  }
},100)
